"""
File upload API routes
Handles GL and AR file uploads with validation
"""
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
import pandas as pd
import aiofiles

from fastapi import APIRouter, UploadFile, File, HTTPException, Depends
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

# Add parent directories to path
sys.path.append(str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent))

from web_app.api.models.upload_models import FileUploadResponse, UploadStatusResponse, FileValidationResult, ValidationError
from web_app.utils.shared_instances import session_manager

# Import from parent directory with proper error handling
try:
    from data_processor import DataProcessor
except ImportError:
    # If running from web_app directory
    sys.path.append(str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent))
    from data_processor import DataProcessor

router = APIRouter()

# Required columns for GL and AR files
REQUIRED_GL_COLUMNS = ['Date', 'Amount']  # Minimum required
OPTIONAL_GL_COLUMNS = ['Account', 'Account Number', 'Description', 'Reference']

REQUIRED_AR_COLUMNS = ['Customer', 'Amount', 'Date']  # Minimum required  
OPTIONAL_AR_COLUMNS = ['Invoice', 'Due Date', 'Days Outstanding']


async def save_upload_file(upload_file: UploadFile, destination: Path) -> None:
    """Save uploaded file to destination"""
    async with aiofiles.open(destination, 'wb') as out_file:
        content = await upload_file.read()
        await out_file.write(content)


def validate_excel_file(file_path: Path, file_type: str) -> FileValidationResult:
    """Validate Excel file structure - basic validation only"""
    result = FileValidationResult(is_valid=True)
    
    try:
        # Read Excel file
        df = pd.read_excel(file_path)
        result.row_count = len(df)
        result.detected_columns = df.columns.tolist()
        
        # Check if file is empty
        if df.empty:
            result.is_valid = False
            result.errors.append(ValidationError(
                field="file",
                message="The uploaded file is empty"
            ))
            return result
        
        # Basic validation - just check we have some columns
        if len(df.columns) < 2:
            result.is_valid = False
            result.errors.append(ValidationError(
                field="file",
                message="File must contain at least 2 columns"
            ))
        
        # Note: Column mapping will be handled in the next step
        result.warnings.append("Column mapping required - proceed to next step")
                
    except Exception as e:
        result.is_valid = False
        result.errors.append(ValidationError(
            field="file",
            message=f"Error reading Excel file: {str(e)}"
        ))
    
    return result


@router.post("/gl", response_model=FileUploadResponse)
async def upload_gl_file(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    """Upload and process GL file"""
    # Validate file type
    if not file.filename.endswith(('.xlsx', '.xls')):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only Excel files (.xlsx, .xls) are supported")
    
    # Create new session
    session_id = session_manager.create_session()
    
    # Save file temporarily
    upload_dir = Path("web_app/static/uploads")
    upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    file_path = upload_dir / f"{session_id}_gl_{file.filename}"
    
    try:
        # Save the uploaded file
        await save_upload_file(file, file_path)
        
        # Validate file structure
        validation_result = validate_excel_file(file_path, "GL")
        if not validation_result.is_valid:
            # Clean up file and session
            file_path.unlink()
            session_manager.delete_session(session_id)
            
            error_messages = [err.message for err in validation_result.errors]
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"File validation failed: {'; '.join(error_messages)}")
        
        # Read and process the file
        df = pd.read_excel(file_path)
        
        # Store in session
        session_manager.set_gl_data(session_id, df, file.filename)
        
        # Clean up temporary file
        file_path.unlink()
        
        return FileUploadResponse(
            success=True,
            session_id=session_id,
            filename=file.filename,
            message=f"GL file uploaded successfully. {len(validation_result.warnings)} warnings.",
            rows_processed=validation_result.row_count,
            columns_detected=validation_result.detected_columns
        )
        
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        # Clean up on error
        if file_path.exists():
            file_path.unlink()
        if session_id:
            session_manager.delete_session(session_id)
        
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error processing file: {str(e)}")


@router.post("/ar", response_model=FileUploadResponse)
async def upload_ar_file(session_id: str, file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    """Upload and process AR file for existing session"""
    # Validate session exists
    session = session_manager.get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    # Validate file type
    if not file.filename.endswith(('.xlsx', '.xls')):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only Excel files (.xlsx, .xls) are supported")
    
    # Save file temporarily
    upload_dir = Path("web_app/static/uploads")
    file_path = upload_dir / f"{session_id}_ar_{file.filename}"
    
    try:
        # Save the uploaded file
        await save_upload_file(file, file_path)
        
        # Validate file structure
        validation_result = validate_excel_file(file_path, "AR")
        if not validation_result.is_valid:
            # Clean up file
            file_path.unlink()
            
            error_messages = [err.message for err in validation_result.errors]
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"File validation failed: {'; '.join(error_messages)}")
        
        # Read and process the file
        df = pd.read_excel(file_path)
        
        # Store in session
        session_manager.set_ar_data(session_id, df, file.filename)
        
        # Clean up temporary file
        file_path.unlink()
        
        return FileUploadResponse(
            success=True,
            session_id=session_id,
            filename=file.filename,
            message=f"AR file uploaded successfully. {len(validation_result.warnings)} warnings.",
            rows_processed=validation_result.row_count,
            columns_detected=validation_result.detected_columns
        )
        
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        # Clean up on error
        if file_path.exists():
            file_path.unlink()
        
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error processing file: {str(e)}")


@router.get("/status/{session_id}", response_model=UploadStatusResponse)

    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    return UploadStatusResponse(
        session_id=session_id,

        ar_uploaded=session.ar_data is not None,

        ar_filename=session.ar_filename,
        gl_rows=len(session.gl_data) if session.gl_data is not None else None,
        ar_rows=len(session.ar_data) if session.ar_data is not None else None,
        ready_for_mapping=session.gl_data is not None,
        created_at=session.created_at,
        last_accessed=session.last_accessed
    )


@router.get("/debug/{session_id}")
async def debug_session_data(session_id: str):
    """Debug endpoint to see session data details"""
    session = session_manager.get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    debug_info = {
        "session_id": session_id,
        "gl_data_exists": session.gl_data is not None,
        "ar_data_exists": session.ar_data is not None,
        "coa_mappings_exists": session.coa_mappings is not None,
        "coa_mappings_count": len(session.coa_mappings) if session.coa_mappings else 0
    }
    
    if session.gl_data is not None:
        debug_info["gl_data_shape"] = list(session.gl_data.shape)
        debug_info["gl_data_columns"] = list(session.gl_data.columns)
        debug_info["gl_data_dtypes"] = {col: str(dtype) for col, dtype in session.gl_data.dtypes.items()}
        
        # Check for standard_account column specifically
        if 'standard_account' in session.gl_data.columns:
            debug_info["standard_account_sample"] = session.gl_data['standard_account'].head(10).tolist()
            debug_info["standard_account_unique_count"] = session.gl_data['standard_account'].nunique()
            debug_info["standard_account_empty_count"] = (session.gl_data['standard_account'] == '').sum()
        
        # Check for account_name column
        if 'account_name' in session.gl_data.columns:
            debug_info["account_name_unique_count"] = session.gl_data['account_name'].nunique()
            debug_info["account_name_sample"] = session.gl_data['account_name'].head(10).tolist()
    
    if session.coa_mappings:
        debug_info["coa_mappings_sample"] = dict(list(session.coa_mappings.items())[:5])
    
    return debug_info


@router.get("/column-info/{session_id}")
async def get_column_info(session_id: str):
    """Get column information and preview data for mapping"""
    session = session_manager.get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    if session.gl_data is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No GL data uploaded for this session")
    
    df = session.gl_data
    
    # Get preview data (first 5 rows) and handle NaN values
    preview_df = df.head(5).fillna('')  # Replace NaN with empty strings
    preview_data = preview_df.to_dict('records')
    
    return {
        "filename": session.gl_filename or "Unknown",
        "row_count": len(df),
        "columns": df.columns.tolist(),
        "preview_data": preview_data
    }


@router.post("/apply-mapping/{session_id}")
async def apply_column_mapping(session_id: str, request: dict):
    """Apply column mapping to transform the data"""
    session = session_manager.get_session(session_id)
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    if session.gl_data is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No GL data uploaded for this session")
    
    try:
        df = session.gl_data.copy()
        mapping = request.get("column_mapping", {})
        amount_type = request.get("amount_type", "single")
        
        # Create standardized DataFrame with DataProcessor-expected columns
        standardized_df = pd.DataFrame()
        
        # Map date column to transaction_date (expected by DataProcessor)
        if "date_column" in mapping:
            standardized_df["transaction_date"] = pd.to_datetime(df[mapping["date_column"]], errors='coerce')
        
        # Map amount column(s) to debit_amount/credit_amount (expected by DataProcessor)
        if amount_type == "single" and "amount_column" in mapping:
            amounts = pd.to_numeric(df[mapping["amount_column"]], errors='coerce').fillna(0)
            # Convert single amount to debit/credit format
            standardized_df["debit_amount"] = amounts.apply(lambda x: x if x > 0 else 0)
            standardized_df["credit_amount"] = amounts.apply(lambda x: -x if x < 0 else 0)
            standardized_df["net_amount"] = amounts
        elif amount_type == "separate":
            debit_col = mapping.get("debit_column")
            credit_col = mapping.get("credit_column")
            
            if debit_col and credit_col:
                standardized_df["debit_amount"] = pd.to_numeric(df[debit_col], errors='coerce').fillna(0)
                standardized_df["credit_amount"] = pd.to_numeric(df[credit_col], errors='coerce').fillna(0)
                standardized_df["net_amount"] = standardized_df["debit_amount"] - standardized_df["credit_amount"]
        
        # Map account column to account_name (expected by DataProcessor)
        if "account_column" in mapping:
            standardized_df["account_name"] = df[mapping["account_column"]].astype(str)
        
        # Map optional columns
        if "description_column" in mapping:
            standardized_df["description"] = df[mapping["description_column"]].astype(str)
        else:
            standardized_df["description"] = ""
        
        if "reference_column" in mapping:
            standardized_df["reference"] = df[mapping["reference_column"]].astype(str)
        else:
            standardized_df["reference"] = ""
        
        # Add required columns expected by DataProcessor
        if "account_number" not in standardized_df.columns:
            # Generate account numbers based on account names
            unique_names = standardized_df["account_name"].unique() if "account_name" in standardized_df.columns else []
            name_to_num = {name: f"ACC{str(i+1).zfill(4)}" for i, name in enumerate(unique_names)}
            if "account_name" in standardized_df.columns:
                standardized_df["account_number"] = standardized_df["account_name"].map(name_to_num)
            else:
                standardized_df["account_number"] = ""
        
        if "transaction_id" not in standardized_df.columns:
            standardized_df["transaction_id"] = standardized_df.index.astype(str)
        
        # Store the standardized data back in session
        session_manager.update_session(session_id, gl_data=standardized_df)
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "message": "Column mapping applied successfully",
            "rows_processed": len(standardized_df),
            "mapped_columns": list(mapping.keys()),
            "expected_columns_created": ["transaction_date", "account_name", "debit_amount", "credit_amount", "net_amount"]
        }
        
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Error applying column mapping: {str(e)}")

"""
FastAPI Web Application for Accounting Analyzer
Main application entry point
"""
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, UploadFile, File, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates
import uvicorn

# Add parent directory to path to import accounting analyzer modules
sys.path.append(str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

# Import API routes
from web_app.api.routes import upload, mapping, financials, analysis

# Import utilities
from web_app.utils.shared_instances import session_manager

# Initialize FastAPI app
app = FastAPI(
    title="Accounting Analyzer",
    description="Web interface for comprehensive accounting analysis",
    version="1.0.0"
)

# Mount static files
app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(Path(__file__).parent / "static")), name="static")

# Setup templates
templates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(Path(__file__).parent / "templates"))

# Include API routers
app.include_router(upload.router, prefix="/api/upload", tags=["upload"])
app.include_router(mapping.router, prefix="/api/mapping", tags=["mapping"])
app.include_router(financials.router, prefix="/api/financials", tags=["financials"])
app.include_router(analysis.router, prefix="/api/analysis", tags=["analysis"])

# Page routes
@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def root(request: Request):
    """Home page - file upload interface"""
    return templates.TemplateResponse("upload.html", {"request": request})

@app.get("/debug/create-test-session")
async def create_test_session():
    """Create a test session with sample GL data"""
    import pandas as pd
    
    # Create sample GL data spanning multiple months
    test_data = {
        'transaction_date': [
            '2024-01-01', '2024-01-01', '2024-01-15', '2024-01-31',  # January
            '2024-02-01', '2024-02-15', '2024-02-28',               # February  
            '2024-03-01', '2024-03-15', '2024-03-31'                # March
        ],
        'account_name': [
            'Service Revenue', 'Accounts Receivable', 'Cash', 'Office Supplies',
            'Service Revenue', 'Cash', 'Rent Expense', 
            'Service Revenue', 'Professional Fees', 'Cash'
        ],
        'description': [
            'Invoice 001', 'Invoice 001', 'Payment received', 'Office supplies',
            'Invoice 002', 'Payment 002', 'Monthly rent',
            'Invoice 003', 'Consulting', 'Payment 003'
        ],
        'debit_amount': [0, 5000, 3000, 150, 0, 2000, 2000, 0, 0, 4000],
        'credit_amount': [5000, 0, 0, 0, 3000, 0, 0, 3500, 1500, 0],
        'account_number': ['4100', '1200', '1000', '6100', '4100', '1000', '7100', '4100', '4200', '1000']
    }
    
    df = pd.DataFrame(test_data)
    df['transaction_date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['transaction_date'])
    df['net_amount'] = df['debit_amount'] - df['credit_amount']
    df['transaction_id'] = df.index.astype(str)
    
    # Add standard COA mapping with proper account structure
    standard_mapping = {
        '4100': {'standard_account': '4100', 'standard_name': 'Service Revenue'},
        '4200': {'standard_account': '4200', 'standard_name': 'Professional Fees'},
        '1200': {'standard_account': '1200', 'standard_name': 'Accounts Receivable - Trade'},
        '1000': {'standard_account': '1000', 'standard_name': 'Cash - Operating Account'},
        '6100': {'standard_account': '6100', 'standard_name': 'Office Supplies'},
        '7100': {'standard_account': '7100', 'standard_name': 'Rent Expense'}
    }
    
    df['standard_account'] = df['account_number'].map(lambda x: standard_mapping.get(x, {}).get('standard_account', x))
    df['standard_name'] = df['account_number'].map(lambda x: standard_mapping.get(x, {}).get('standard_name', ''))
    
    # Create session
    session_id = session_manager.create_session()
    session_manager.set_gl_data(session_id, df, "test_data.xlsx")
    
    return {
        "session_id": session_id,
        "message": "Test session created with sample data",
        "redirect_url": f"/dashboard/{session_id}"
    }




# Startup event
@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup_event():
    """Initialize application on startup"""
    # Ensure upload directory exists
    upload_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "static" / "uploads"
    upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Clean old sessions on startup
    session_manager.cleanup_expired_sessions()

# Shutdown event
@app.on_event("shutdown")
async def shutdown_event():
    """Cleanup on shutdown"""
    # Clean up any temporary files
    upload_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "static" / "uploads"
    if upload_dir.exists():
        for file in upload_dir.iterdir():
            if file.is_file():
                try:
                    file.unlink()
                except Exception:
                    pass

if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Run the application
    uvicorn.run(
        "main:app",
        host="0.0.0.0",
        port=8000,
        reload=True,
        log_level="info"
    )

/* Custom CSS for Accounting Analyzer Web App */

// Main JavaScript for Accounting Analyzer Web App

// Global utilities
const formatCurrency = (amount) => {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
        style: 'currency',
        currency: 'USD',
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits: 0
    }).format(amount);
};

const formatPercent = (value) => {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
        style: 'percent',
        minimumFractionDigits: 1,
        maximumFractionDigits: 1
    }).format(value / 100);
};

// Show loading overlay
const showLoading = (message = 'Loading...') => {
    const loadingHtml = `
        <div class="loading-overlay">
            <div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status">
                <span class="visually-hidden">Loading...</span>
            </div>
            <p class="mt-3">${message}</p>
        </div>
    `;
    $('body').append(loadingHtml);
};

// Hide loading overlay
const hideLoading = () => {
    $('.loading-overlay').remove();
};

// Show toast notification
const showToast = (message, type = 'info') => {
    const toastHtml = `
        <div class="toast align-items-center text-white bg-${type} border-0" role="alert">
            <div class="d-flex">
                <div class="toast-body">
                    ${message}
                </div>
                <button type="button" class="btn-close btn-close-white me-2 m-auto" data-bs-dismiss="toast"></button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
    
    const toastContainer = $('#toastContainer');
    if (!toastContainer.length) {
        $('body').append('<div id="toastContainer" class="toast-container position-fixed bottom-0 end-0 p-3"></div>');
    }
    
    const toastElement = $(toastHtml);
    $('#toastContainer').append(toastElement);
    
    const toast = new bootstrap.Toast(toastElement[0]);
    toast.show();
    
    // Remove after hidden
    toastElement.on('hidden.bs.toast', function() {
        $(this).remove();
    });
};

// Handle API errors
const handleApiError = (xhr) => {
    const error = xhr.responseJSON?.detail || 'An unexpected error occurred';
    showToast(error, 'danger');
};

// Session management
const checkSession = async (sessionId) => {
    try {
        const response = await $.get(`/api/upload/status/${sessionId}`);
        return response;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Session check failed:', error);
        return null;
    }
};

// File size formatter
const formatFileSize = (bytes) => {
    if (bytes === 0) return '0 Bytes';
    const k = 1024;
    const sizes = ['Bytes', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
    const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
    return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
};

// Debounce function
const debounce = (func, wait) => {
    let timeout;
    return function executedFunction(...args) {
        const later = () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            func(...args);
        };
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
    };
};

// Initialize tooltips
$(document).ready(() => {
    // Initialize Bootstrap tooltips
    const tooltipTriggerList = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]'));
    tooltipTriggerList.map(function (tooltipTriggerEl) {
        return new bootstrap.Tooltip(tooltipTriggerEl);
    });
    
    // Initialize popovers
    const popoverTriggerList = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="popover"]'));
    popoverTriggerList.map(function (popoverTriggerEl) {
        return new bootstrap.Popover(popoverTriggerEl);
    });
});

// Export functions for use in other scripts
window.accountingAnalyzer = {
    formatCurrency,
    formatPercent,
    showLoading,
    hideLoading,
    showToast,
    handleApiError,
    checkSession,
    formatFileSize,
    debounce
};

            <button class="btn btn-success" id="newAnalysisBtn">
                <i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Start New Analysis
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    const sessionId = '{{ session_id }}';
    let currentPhase = 1;
    let allAnalysisData = {};

    // Phase navigation
    $('.phase-nav .nav-link').on('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        $('.phase-nav .nav-link').removeClass('active');
        $(this).addClass('active');
        currentPhase = $(this).data('phase');
        displayPhaseAnalysis(currentPhase);
    });

    // Load all analysis data
    async function loadAnalysisData() {
        try {
            // Load overview
            const overviewResponse = await $.get(`/api/analysis/overview/${sessionId}`);
            allAnalysisData = overviewResponse;
            
            // Update summary counts
            const summary = overviewResponse.summary;
            $('#totalFlags').text(summary.total);
            $('#highRiskCount').text(summary.high);
            $('#mediumRiskCount').text(summary.medium);
            $('#lowRiskCount').text(summary.low);
            
            // Display first phase by default
            displayPhaseAnalysis(1);
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load analysis:', error);
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to load analysis data', 'danger');
            $('#analysisContent').html(`
                <div class="alert alert-danger">
                    <h5><i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Error Loading Analysis</h5>
                    <p>Failed to load analysis data. Please try refreshing the page or contact support.</p>
                </div>
            `);
        }
    }

    // Display phase analysis with real data
    async function displayPhaseAnalysis(phaseId) {
        try {
            // Show loading spinner
            $('#analysisContent').html(`
                <div class="text-center py-5">
                    <div class="spinner-border text-primary" role="status">
                        <span class="visually-hidden">Loading...</span>
                    </div>
                    <h5 class="mt-3">Loading phase analysis...</h5>
                </div>
            `);

            // Load phase-specific data
            const phaseResponse = await $.get(`/api/analysis/phase/${sessionId}/${phaseId}`);
            const phaseData = phaseResponse;

            // Build phase content
            let phaseHtml = `
                <div class="phase-content">
                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
                        <div>
                            <h2 class="mb-2">${phaseData.explanation.title}</h2>
                            <p class="text-muted mb-0">${phaseData.explanation.description}</p>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-right">
                            <div class="d-flex gap-2">
                                <span class="badge bg-danger">${phaseData.high_risk_count} High</span>
                                <span class="badge bg-warning">${phaseData.medium_risk_count} Medium</span>
                                <span class="badge bg-success">${phaseData.low_risk_count} Low</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Search and Filter -->
                    <div class="row mb-4">
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="input-group">
                                <span class="input-group-text"><i class="fas fa-search"></i></span>
                                <input type="text" class="form-control" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search flags...">
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-3">
                            <select class="form-select" id="severityFilter">
                                <option value="">All Severities</option>
                                <option value="High">High Risk</option>
                                <option value="Medium">Medium Risk</option>
                                <option value="Low">Low Risk</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-3">
                            <button class="btn btn-outline-primary" onclick="exportPhaseAnalysis(${phaseId})">
                                <i class="fas fa-download"></i> Export Phase
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Key Metrics -->
                    <div class="row mb-4">
                        <div class="col-12">
                            <h5>Key Metrics Analyzed</h5>
                            <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                ${phaseData.explanation.metrics.map(metric => `
                                    <span class="badge bg-light text-dark">${metric}</span>
                                `).join('')}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Analysis Results -->
                    <div id="phaseResults">
            `;

            // Display flags
            if (phaseData.flags && phaseData.flags.length > 0) {
                phaseData.flags.forEach(flag => {
                    const severityClass = flag.severity.toLowerCase() === 'high' ? 'analysis-severity-high' : 
                                        flag.severity.toLowerCase() === 'medium' ? 'analysis-severity-medium' : 'analysis-severity-low';
                    const badgeClass = flag.severity.toLowerCase() === 'high' ? 'bg-danger' : 
                                      flag.severity.toLowerCase() === 'medium' ? 'bg-warning' : 'bg-success';
                    
                    phaseHtml += `
                        <div class="analysis-card card ${severityClass} mb-3" data-severity="${flag.severity}" data-flag="${flag.flag}">
                            <div class="card-body">
                                <h5 class="card-title d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start">
                                    <span>
                                        <span class="badge ${badgeClass} me-2">${flag.severity} Risk</span>
                                        ${flag.flag}
                                    </span>
                                    ${flag.amount ? `<span class="text-muted small">${formatCurrency(flag.amount)}</span>` : ''}
                                </h5>
                                <div class="card-text">
                                    <div class="mb-2">
                                        <strong>Details:</strong> ${flag.detail}
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="mb-2">
                                        <strong>Impact:</strong> ${flag.impact}
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="mb-2">
                                        <strong>Recommended Action:</strong> ${flag.action}
                                    </div>
                                    ${flag.period ? `<div class="text-muted small">Period: ${flag.period}</div>` : ''}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
            } else {
                phaseHtml += `
                    <div class="alert alert-success">
                        <h5><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> No Issues Found</h5>
                        <p>No red flags were identified in this analysis phase. This is a positive indicator for your business.</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            }

            phaseHtml += `
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;

            $('#analysisContent').html(phaseHtml);

            // Add search functionality
            $('#searchInput').on('input', function() {
                filterFlags();
            });

            $('#severityFilter').on('change', function() {
                filterFlags();
            });

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load phase analysis:', error);
            $('#analysisContent').html(`
                <div class="alert alert-danger">
                    <h5><i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Error Loading Phase</h5>
                    <p>Failed to load phase analysis. Please try again.</p>
                </div>
            `);
        }
    }

    // Filter flags based on search and severity
    function filterFlags() {
        const searchTerm = $('#searchInput').val().toLowerCase();
        const severity = $('#severityFilter').val();

        $('.analysis-card').each(function() {
            const flagText = $(this).data('flag').toLowerCase();
            const flagSeverity = $(this).data('severity');
            const matchesSearch = !searchTerm || flagText.includes(searchTerm);
            const matchesSeverity = !severity || flagSeverity === severity;

            if (matchesSearch && matchesSeverity) {
                $(this).show();
            } else {
                $(this).hide();
            }
        });
    }

    // Format currency
    function formatCurrency(amount) {
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
            style: 'currency',
            currency: 'USD'
        }).format(amount);
    }

    // Export phase analysis
    window.exportPhaseAnalysis = function(phaseId) {
        accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Exporting phase analysis...', 'info');
        window.open(`/api/analysis/export/${sessionId}?format=excel&phase=${phaseId}`, '_blank');
    };

    // Export functionality
    $('#exportAnalysisBtn').on('click', function() {
        const exportModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('exportModal'));
        exportModal.show();
    });

    $('#confirmExportBtn').on('click', function() {
        const format = $('input[name="exportFormat"]:checked').val();
        accountingAnalyzer.showToast(`Exporting analysis as ${format.toUpperCase()}...`, 'info');
        // Implement actual export functionality
        bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('exportModal')).hide();
    });

    // New analysis button
    $('#newAnalysisBtn').on('click', function() {
        if (confirm('Start a new analysis? This will return you to the upload page.')) {
            window.location.href = '/';
        }
    });

    // Initialize
    loadAnalysisData();
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    const sessionId = '{{ session_id }}';
    const loadingModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('loadingModal'));
    let fileData = {};

    // Load file data and populate form
    async function loadFileData() {
        try {
            const response = await $.get(`/api/upload/column-info/${sessionId}`);
            fileData = response;
            
            // Update file info
            $('#fileName').text(fileData.filename);
            $('#rowCount').text(fileData.row_count);
            $('#columnCount').text(fileData.columns.length);
            
            // Populate column dropdowns
            populateColumnDropdowns(fileData.columns);
            
            // Auto-suggest mappings
            autoSuggestMappings(fileData.columns);
            
            // Show preview
            showDataPreview(fileData.preview_data, fileData.columns);
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load file data:', error);
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to load file data', 'danger');
        }
    }

    // Populate all column dropdown options
    function populateColumnDropdowns(columns) {
        const dropdowns = ['dateColumn', 'amountColumn', 'accountColumn', 'descriptionColumn', 'referenceColumn', 'debitColumn', 'creditColumn'];
        
        dropdowns.forEach(dropdownId => {
            const select = $(`#${dropdownId}`);
            select.empty().append('<option value="">-- Select Column --</option>');
            
            columns.forEach(column => {
                select.append(`<option value="${column}">${column}</option>`);
            });
        });
    }

    // Auto-suggest mappings based on column names
    function autoSuggestMappings(columns) {
        const suggestions = {
            date: ['date', 'transaction_date', 'trans_date', 'posting_date'],
            amount: ['amount', 'total', 'net_amount'],
            account: ['account', 'account_name', 'account_number', 'gl_account'],
            description: ['description', 'memo', 'narration', 'details'],
            reference: ['reference', 'ref', 'reference_number', 'transaction_id'],
            debit: ['debit', 'debit_amount', 'dr'],
            credit: ['credit', 'credit_amount', 'cr']
        };

        // Auto-select best matches
        Object.keys(suggestions).forEach(field => {
            const fieldColumn = field === 'account' ? 'accountColumn' : 
                               field === 'description' ? 'descriptionColumn' :
                               field === 'reference' ? 'referenceColumn' :
                               field + 'Column';
            
            const bestMatch = findBestMatch(columns, suggestions[field]);
            if (bestMatch) {
                $(`#${fieldColumn}`).val(bestMatch);
            }
        });

        // Check if we have separate debit/credit columns
        const hasDebit = findBestMatch(columns, suggestions.debit);
        const hasCredit = findBestMatch(columns, suggestions.credit);
        
        if (hasDebit && hasCredit) {
            $('#separateAmounts').prop('checked', true);
            $('#separateAmountOptions').show();
            $('#debitColumn').val(hasDebit);
            $('#creditColumn').val(hasCredit);
            $('#amountColumn').val(''); // Clear single amount
        }
    }

    // Find best column name match
    function findBestMatch(columns, keywords) {
        for (let keyword of keywords) {
            const match = columns.find(col => col.toLowerCase().includes(keyword.toLowerCase()));
            if (match) return match;
        }
        return null;
    }

    // Show data preview
    function showDataPreview(previewData, columns) {
        const headerRow = $('#previewHeaders');
        const bodyContainer = $('#previewBody');
        
        // Clear existing content
        headerRow.empty();
        bodyContainer.empty();
        
        // Add headers
        columns.forEach(column => {
            headerRow.append(`<th>${column}</th>`);
        });
        
        // Add preview rows (first 5 rows)
        previewData.slice(0, 5).forEach(row => {
            const rowHtml = columns.map(column => `<td>${row[column] || ''}</td>`).join('');
            bodyContainer.append(`<tr>${rowHtml}</tr>`);
        });
    }

    // Handle amount type radio button change
    $('input[name="amountType"]').change(function() {
        if ($(this).val() === 'separate') {
            $('#separateAmountOptions').show();
            $('#amountColumn').val('').prop('required', false);
            $('#debitColumn, #creditColumn').prop('required', true);
        } else {
            $('#separateAmountOptions').hide();
            $('#amountColumn').prop('required', true);
            $('#debitColumn, #creditColumn').val('').prop('required', false);
        }
    });

    // Confirm mapping
    $('#confirmMappingBtn').on('click', async function() {
        const formData = new FormData($('#columnMappingForm')[0]);
        const mapping = {};
        
        // Collect form data
        for (let [key, value] of formData.entries()) {
            if (value) mapping[key] = value;
        }
        
        // Validate required fields
        const amountType = $('input[name="amountType"]:checked').val();
        if (!mapping.date_column) {
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Please select a date column', 'warning');
            return;
        }
        
        if (amountType === 'single' && !mapping.amount_column) {
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Please select an amount column', 'warning');
            return;
        }
        
        if (amountType === 'separate' && (!mapping.debit_column || !mapping.credit_column)) {
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Please select both debit and credit columns', 'warning');
            return;
        }

        try {
            loadingModal.show();
            $('#loadingMessage').text('Applying column mapping...');
            
            const response = await $.ajax({
                url: `/api/upload/apply-mapping/${sessionId}`,
                type: 'POST',
                data: JSON.stringify({
                    column_mapping: mapping,
                    amount_type: amountType
                }),
                contentType: 'application/json'
            });
            
            if (response.success) {
                accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Column mapping applied successfully', 'success');
                // Redirect to mapping page
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.location.href = `/mapping/${sessionId}`;
                }, 1000);
            }
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to apply mapping:', error);
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to apply column mapping', 'danger');
            loadingModal.hide();
        }
    });

    // Initialize
    loadFileData();
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    const sessionId = '{{ session_id }}';
    let currentView = 'original';

    // Toggle view handlers
    $('#originalViewBtn').on('click', function() {
        currentView = 'original';
        $(this).addClass('active').removeClass('btn-outline-primary').addClass('btn-primary');
        $('#adjustedViewBtn').removeClass('active').removeClass('btn-primary').addClass('btn-outline-primary');
        $('#addbacksSection').hide();
        loadFinancials();
    });

    $('#adjustedViewBtn').on('click', function() {
        currentView = 'adjusted';
        $(this).addClass('active').removeClass('btn-outline-primary').addClass('btn-primary');
        $('#originalViewBtn').removeClass('active').removeClass('btn-primary').addClass('btn-outline-primary');
        $('#addbacksSection').show();
        loadFinancials();
    });

    // Load financial data
    async function loadFinancials() {
        try {
            // Load P&L
            const plResponse = await $.get(`/api/financials/pl/${sessionId}?adjusted=${currentView === 'adjusted'}`);
            renderPL(plResponse.data);

            // Load Balance Sheet
            const bsResponse = await $.get(`/api/financials/bs/${sessionId}?adjusted=${currentView === 'adjusted'}`);
            renderBS(bsResponse.data);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load financials:', error);
            accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to load financial data', 'danger');
        }
    }

    // Render P&L Statement
    function renderPL(data) {
        if (!data || Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
            $('#plStatement').html(`
                <div class="text-center text-muted py-5">
                    <i class="fas fa-chart-line fa-3x mb-3"></i>
                    <p>No P&L data available</p>
                </div>
            `);
            return;
        }

        const html = `
            <div class="financial-statement-header">
                <h6 class="mb-3">Profit & Loss Statement</h6>
                <p class="text-muted small mb-3">Period: ${data.period || 'Current'}</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="financial-table">
                <table class="table table-sm">
                    <tbody>
                        <tr class="revenue-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>REVENUE</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.revenue?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="expense-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>COST OF GOODS SOLD</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.cogs?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="total-row">
                            <td class="total-label"><strong>GROSS PROFIT</strong></td>
                            <td class="total-amount text-right"><strong>${data.gross_profit?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="expense-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>OPERATING EXPENSES</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.operating_expenses?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="income-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>OTHER INCOME</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.other_income?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="expense-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>OTHER EXPENSES</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.other_expenses?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="net-income-row ${(data.net_income?.value || 0) >= 0 ? 'positive' : 'negative'}">
                            <td class="net-income-label"><strong>NET INCOME</strong></td>
                            <td class="net-income-amount text-right"><strong>${data.net_income?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            
            <div class="export-buttons mt-3">
                <button class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm" onclick="exportPL()">
                    <i class="fas fa-file-excel"></i> Export to Excel
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
        
        $('#plStatement').html(html);
    }

    // Render Balance Sheet
    function renderBS(data) {
        if (!data || Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
            $('#balanceSheet').html(`
                <div class="text-center text-muted py-5">
                    <i class="fas fa-balance-scale fa-3x mb-3"></i>
                    <p>No Balance Sheet data available</p>
                </div>
            `);
            return;
        }

        const isBalanced = data.is_balanced;
        const balanceClass = isBalanced ? 'balanced' : 'unbalanced';
        
        const html = `
            <div class="financial-statement-header">
                <h6 class="mb-3">Balance Sheet</h6>
                <p class="text-muted small mb-3">Period: ${data.period || 'Current'}</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="financial-table">
                <table class="table table-sm">
                    <tbody>
                        <tr class="asset-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>ASSETS</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.assets?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="total-row">
                            <td class="total-label"><strong>TOTAL ASSETS</strong></td>
                            <td class="total-amount text-right"><strong>${data.total_assets?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="liability-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>LIABILITIES</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.liabilities?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="equity-section">
                            <td class="section-header"><strong>EQUITY</strong></td>
                            <td class="amount text-right"><strong>${data.equity_base?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="equity-section">
                            <td class="indent-1">Retained Earnings</td>
                            <td class="amount text-right">${data.retained_earnings?.formatted || '$0.00'}</td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="total-row">
                            <td class="total-label"><strong>TOTAL EQUITY</strong></td>
                            <td class="total-amount text-right"><strong>${data.total_equity?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="spacer-row">
                            <td colspan="2"></td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="final-total-row ${balanceClass}">
                            <td class="final-total-label"><strong>TOTAL LIABILITIES + EQUITY</strong></td>
                            <td class="final-total-amount text-right"><strong>${data.total_liabilities_and_equity?.formatted || '$0.00'}</strong></td>
                        </tr>
                        ${!isBalanced ? `
                        <tr class="balance-check-row text-danger">
                            <td class="balance-check-label"><small>Balance Check Difference</small></td>
                            <td class="balance-check-amount text-right"><small>${data.balance_check?.formatted || '$0.00'}</small></td>
                        </tr>
                        ` : ''}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
            
            <div class="export-buttons mt-3">
                <button class="btn btn-outline-success btn-sm" onclick="exportBS()">
                    <i class="fas fa-file-excel"></i> Export to Excel
                </button>
            </div>
        `;
        
        $('#balanceSheet').html(html);
    }

    // Export functions
    window.exportPL = function() {
        window.open(`/api/financials/export/pl/${sessionId}?adjusted=${currentView === 'adjusted'}`, '_blank');
    };

    window.exportBS = function() {
        window.open(`/api/financials/export/bs/${sessionId}?adjusted=${currentView === 'adjusted'}`, '_blank');
    };

    // Load add-backs transactions
    async function loadAddbacks() {
        try {
            const response = await $.get(`/api/financials/transactions/${sessionId}`);
            renderAddbacks(response);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load add-backs:', error);
        }
    }

    // Render add-backs section
    function renderAddbacks(data) {
        // One-time expenses
        const oneTimeHtml = data.one_time_expenses.map(transaction => `
            <div class="form-check mb-2">
                <input class="form-check-input addback-checkbox" type="checkbox" 
                       id="one_time_${transaction.transaction_id}" 
                       data-category="one_time" 
                       data-transaction-id="${transaction.transaction_id}">
                <label class="form-check-label" for="one_time_${transaction.transaction_id}">
                    <div class="transaction-item">
                        <div class="transaction-desc">${transaction.description}</div>
                        <div class="transaction-details">
                            <small class="text-muted">${transaction.date} • ${transaction.account_name}</small>
                            <span class="transaction-amount">${transaction.formatted_amount}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </label>
            </div>
        `).join('');

        // Owner adjustments
        const ownerHtml = data.owner_adjustments.map(transaction => `
            <div class="form-check mb-2">
                <input class="form-check-input addback-checkbox" type="checkbox" 
                       id="owner_${transaction.transaction_id}" 
                       data-category="owner_adj" 
                       data-transaction-id="${transaction.transaction_id}">
                <label class="form-check-label" for="owner_${transaction.transaction_id}">
                    <div class="transaction-item">
                        <div class="transaction-desc">${transaction.description}</div>
                        <div class="transaction-details">
                            <small class="text-muted">${transaction.date} • ${transaction.account_name}</small>
                            <span class="transaction-amount">${transaction.formatted_amount}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </label>
            </div>
        `).join('');

        // Non-recurring revenue
        const nonRecurringHtml = data.non_recurring_revenue.map(transaction => `
            <div class="form-check mb-2">
                <input class="form-check-input addback-checkbox" type="checkbox" 
                       id="non_recurring_${transaction.transaction_id}" 
                       data-category="non_recurring" 
                       data-transaction-id="${transaction.transaction_id}">
                <label class="form-check-label" for="non_recurring_${transaction.transaction_id}">
                    <div class="transaction-item">
                        <div class="transaction-desc">${transaction.description}</div>
                        <div class="transaction-details">
                            <small class="text-muted">${transaction.date} • ${transaction.account_name}</small>
                            <span class="transaction-amount">${transaction.formatted_amount}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </label>
            </div>
        `).join('');

        $('#oneTimeExpenses').html(oneTimeHtml || '<p class="text-muted">No one-time expenses found</p>');
        $('#ownerAdjustments').html(ownerHtml || '<p class="text-muted">No owner adjustments found</p>');
        $('#nonRecurringRevenue').html(nonRecurringHtml || '<p class="text-muted">No non-recurring revenue found</p>');

        // Add event listeners for checkboxes
        $('.addback-checkbox').on('change', function() {
            updateAddbacks();
        });
    }

    // Update add-backs and refresh financials
    async function updateAddbacks() {
        const selectedAddbacks = {
            one_time: [],
            owner_adj: [],
            non_recurring: []
        };

        $('.addback-checkbox:checked').each(function() {
            const category = $(this).data('category');
            const transactionId = $(this).data('transaction-id');
            selectedAddbacks[category].push(transactionId);
        });

        try {
            await $.post(`/api/financials/addbacks/${sessionId}`, selectedAddbacks);
            // Refresh financials if in adjusted view
            if (currentView === 'adjusted') {
                loadFinancials();
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to update add-backs:', error);
        }
    }

    // Load add-backs when adjusted view is shown
    $('#adjustedViewBtn').on('click', function() {
        loadAddbacks();
    });

    // Initialize
    loadFinancials();
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    const sessionId = '{{ session_id }}';
    let mappings = [];
    let standardAccounts = [];
    let modifiedMappings = {};
    const loadingModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('loadingModal'));

    // Load standard accounts for dropdown
    async function loadStandardAccounts() {
        try {
            const response = await $.get('/api/mapping/standard-accounts');
            standardAccounts = response;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load standard accounts:', error);
            alert('Failed to load standard accounts');
        }
    }

    // Load mapping suggestions
    async function loadMappings() {
        try {
            const response = await $.get(`/api/mapping/suggestions/${sessionId}`);
            mappings = response.mappings;
            
            // Update summary counts
            $('#totalAccounts').text(response.mappings.length);
            $('#highConfidence').text(response.high_confidence_count);
            $('#mediumConfidence').text(response.medium_confidence_count);
            $('#lowConfidence').text(response.low_confidence_count + response.unmapped_count);
            
            // Render table
            renderMappingTable();
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to load mappings:', error);
            alert('Failed to load mappings');
        }
    }

    // Render mapping table
    function renderMappingTable() {
        const tbody = $('#mappingTableBody');
        tbody.empty();
        
        mappings.forEach((mapping, index) => {
            const percentage = Math.round(mapping.confidence_score * 100);
            const confidenceBadge = `<span class="badge bg-success">${percentage}%</span>`;
            const isModified = mapping.is_user_modified || modifiedMappings[mapping.original_account];
            
            const row = $(`
                <tr>
                    <td>${mapping.original_account}</td>
                    <td>${confidenceBadge}</td>
                    <td>
                        <select class="form-select form-select-sm mapping-select" 
                                data-account="${mapping.original_account}" 
                                data-index="${index}">
                            <option value="">-- Select Account --</option>
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        ${isModified ? '<span class="badge bg-info">Modified</span>' : ''}
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `);
            
            tbody.append(row);
            
            // Populate dropdown with all standard accounts
            const select = row.find('.mapping-select');
            populateAccountDropdown(select, mapping.suggested_mapping);
        });
        
        // Attach change handlers
        $('.mapping-select').on('change', handleMappingChange);
    }

    // Get confidence badge HTML
    function getConfidenceBadge(level, score) {
        const percentage = Math.round(score * 100);
        const classes = {
            'high': 'confidence-high',
            'medium': 'confidence-medium',
            'low': 'confidence-low',
            'manual': 'confidence-manual'
        };
        
        return `<span class="badge confidence-badge ${classes[level]}">${percentage}%</span>`;
    }

    // Populate account dropdown
    function populateAccountDropdown(select, currentValue) {
        // Group accounts by category
        const grouped = {};
        standardAccounts.forEach(account => {
            if (!grouped[account.category]) {
                grouped[account.category] = [];
            }
            grouped[account.category].push(account);
        });
        
        // Add options grouped by category
        Object.keys(grouped).sort().forEach(category => {
            const optgroup = $(`<optgroup label="${category}">`);
            
            grouped[category].forEach(account => {
                const option = $(`<option value="${account.account_name}">${account.account_name}</option>`);
                if (account.account_name === currentValue) {
                    option.prop('selected', true);
                }
                optgroup.append(option);
            });
            
            select.append(optgroup);
        });
        
        // Add "Unmapped" option
        const unmappedOption = $('<option value="Unmapped">Unmapped</option>');
        if (currentValue === 'Unmapped') {
            unmappedOption.prop('selected', true);
        }
        select.append(unmappedOption);
    }

    // Handle mapping change
    function handleMappingChange(e) {
        const account = $(e.target).data('account');
        const newMapping = $(e.target).val();
        const index = $(e.target).data('index');
        
        if (newMapping) {
            modifiedMappings[account] = newMapping;
            mappings[index].suggested_mapping = newMapping;
            mappings[index].is_user_modified = true;
            
            // Update modified badge
            $(e.target).closest('tr').find('td:last').html('<span class="badge bg-info">Modified</span>');
        }
    }

    // Save changes
    $('#saveChangesBtn').on('click', async function() {
        if (Object.keys(modifiedMappings).length === 0) {
            alert('No changes to save');
            return;
        }
        
        const updates = Object.entries(modifiedMappings).map(([account, mapping]) => ({
            original_account: account,
            new_mapping: mapping
        }));
        
        try {
            loadingModal.show();
            await $.ajax({
                url: `/api/mapping/update-bulk/${sessionId}`,
                type: 'POST',
                data: JSON.stringify({ mappings: updates }),
                contentType: 'application/json'
            });
            
            alert('Mappings saved successfully');
            modifiedMappings = {};
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to save mappings:', error);
            alert('Failed to save mappings');
        } finally {
            loadingModal.hide();
        }
    });

    // Confirm mappings and continue
    $('#confirmMappingsBtn').on('click', async function() {
        // First save any pending changes
        if (Object.keys(modifiedMappings).length > 0) {
            await $('#saveChangesBtn').click();
        }
        
        try {
            loadingModal.show();
            $('#loadingMessage').text('Confirming mappings...');
            
            const response = await $.ajax({
                url: `/api/mapping/confirm/${sessionId}`,
                type: 'POST',
                data: JSON.stringify({ confirm: true }),
                contentType: 'application/json',
                timeout: 30000  // 30 second timeout
            });
            
            // Always hide modal first
            loadingModal.hide();
            
            if (response.success) {
                // Show success message with toast instead of alert
                if (window.accountingAnalyzer && window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast) {
                    window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Mappings confirmed successfully! Redirecting...', 'success');
                } else {
                    console.log('Mappings confirmed successfully');
                }
                
                // Redirect immediately without delay
                window.location.href = `/dashboard/${sessionId}`;
            } else {
                // Show error message
                const errorMsg = response.message || 'Unknown error occurred';
                if (window.accountingAnalyzer && window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast) {
                    window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to confirm mappings: ' + errorMsg, 'danger');
                } else {
                    alert('Failed to confirm mappings: ' + errorMsg);
                }
            }
            
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to confirm mappings:', error);
            
            // Ensure modal is hidden
            loadingModal.hide();
            
            // Show error message
            const errorMsg = error.responseJSON?.detail || error.message || 'Network error occurred';
            if (window.accountingAnalyzer && window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast) {
                window.accountingAnalyzer.showToast('Failed to confirm mappings: ' + errorMsg, 'danger');
            } else {
                alert('Failed to confirm mappings: ' + errorMsg);
            }
        }
    });

    // Initialize
    async function init() {
        await loadStandardAccounts();
        await loadMappings();
    }

    init();
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    let sessionId = null;
    const loadingModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('loadingModal'));
    
    // GL Upload Handler
    $('#glUploadForm').on('submit', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        const formData = new FormData(this);
        const fileInput = $('#glFile')[0];
        
        if (!fileInput.files.length) {
            alert('Please select a file');
            return;
        }
        
        // Show loading
        $('#loadingMessage').text('Uploading and validating GL file...');
        loadingModal.show();
        $('#glUploadBtn').prop('disabled', true);
        
        // Upload file
        $.ajax({
            url: '/api/upload/gl',
            type: 'POST',
            data: formData,
            processData: false,
            contentType: false,
            success: function(response) {
                loadingModal.hide();
                sessionId = response.session_id;
                $('#sessionId').val(sessionId);
                
                // Show success message
                $('#glUploadStatus').show();
                $('#glStatusMessage').html(`
                    <i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> <strong>Success!</strong><br>
                    File: ${response.filename}<br>
                    Rows processed: ${response.rows_processed}<br>
                    Columns detected: ${response.columns_detected.join(', ')}
                `);
                $('#glUploadStatus .alert').removeClass('alert-danger').addClass('alert-success');
                
                // Show AR upload section
                $('#arUploadSection').show();
                $('#continueSection').show();
                $('#continueBtn').attr('href', `/column-mapping/${sessionId}`);
                
                // Disable GL form
                $('#glFile').prop('disabled', true);
            },
            error: function(xhr) {
                loadingModal.hide();
                $('#glUploadBtn').prop('disabled', false);
                
                // Show error message
                $('#glUploadStatus').show();
                const error = xhr.responseJSON?.detail || 'An error occurred while uploading the file';
                $('#glStatusMessage').html(`
                    <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> <strong>Error!</strong><br>
                    ${error}
                `);
                $('#glUploadStatus .alert').removeClass('alert-success').addClass('alert-danger');
            }
        });
    });
    
    // AR Upload Handler
    $('#arUploadForm').on('submit', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        
        const formData = new FormData(this);
        const fileInput = $('#arFile')[0];
        
        if (!fileInput.files.length) {
            alert('Please select a file');
            return;
        }
        
        // Show loading
        $('#loadingMessage').text('Uploading and validating AR file...');
        loadingModal.show();
        $('#arUploadBtn').prop('disabled', true);
        
        // Upload file
        $.ajax({
            url: `/api/upload/ar?session_id=${sessionId}`,
            type: 'POST',
            data: formData,
            processData: false,
            contentType: false,
            success: function(response) {
                loadingModal.hide();
                
                // Show success message
                $('#arUploadStatus').show();
                $('#arStatusMessage').html(`
                    <i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> <strong>Success!</strong><br>
                    File: ${response.filename}<br>
                    Rows processed: ${response.rows_processed}<br>
                    Columns detected: ${response.columns_detected.join(', ')}
                `);
                $('#arUploadStatus .alert').removeClass('alert-danger').addClass('alert-success');
                
                // Disable AR form
                $('#arFile').prop('disabled', true);
                $('#skipArBtn').hide();
            },
            error: function(xhr) {
                loadingModal.hide();
                $('#arUploadBtn').prop('disabled', false);
                
                // Show error message
                $('#arUploadStatus').show();
                const error = xhr.responseJSON?.detail || 'An error occurred while uploading the file';
                $('#arStatusMessage').html(`
                    <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> <strong>Error!</strong><br>
                    ${error}
                `);
                $('#arUploadStatus .alert').removeClass('alert-success').addClass('alert-danger');
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Skip AR Button Handler
    $('#skipArBtn').on('click', function() {
        window.location.href = `/column-mapping/${sessionId}`;
    });
});
</script>

================================================================================
FILE: analyzer/web_app\utils\session_manager.py
================================================================================
"""
Session Manager for handling user sessions
Stores session data in memory for MVP
"""
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, Optional, Any
import pandas as pd
from pydantic import BaseModel


class SessionData(BaseModel):
    """Session data model"""
    session_id: str
    gl_data: Optional[Any] = None  # Will be DataFrame, but can't serialize directly
    ar_data: Optional[Any] = None  # Will be DataFrame, but can't serialize directly
    coa_mappings: Dict[str, str] = {}
    addbacks: Dict[str, list] = {
        "one_time": [],
        "owner_adj": [],
        "non_recurring": []
    }
    financials: Dict = {}  # Original and adjusted P&L/BS
    analysis_results: Dict = {}  # All 5 phases results
    created_at: datetime = datetime.now()
    last_accessed: datetime = datetime.now()
    gl_filename: Optional[str] = None
    ar_filename: Optional[str] = None
    
    class Config:
        arbitrary_types_allowed = True


class SessionManager:
    """Manages user sessions in memory"""
    
    def __init__(self, session_timeout_hours: int = 24):
        self.sessions: Dict[str, SessionData] = {}
        self.session_timeout = timedelta(hours=session_timeout_hours)
    
    def create_session(self) -> str:
        """Create a new session and return session ID"""
        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        self.sessions[session_id] = SessionData(session_id=session_id)
        return session_id
    
    def get_session(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[SessionData]:
        """Get session data by ID"""
        session = self.sessions.get(session_id)
        if session:
            # Update last accessed time
            session.last_accessed = datetime.now()
            # Check if session has expired
            if datetime.now() - session.created_at > self.session_timeout:
                self.delete_session(session_id)
                return None
        return session
    
    def update_session(self, session_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update session data"""
        session = self.get_session(session_id)
        if not session:
            return False
        
        for key, value in kwargs.items():
            if hasattr(session, key):
                setattr(session, key, value)
        
        session.last_accessed = datetime.now()
        return True
    
    def delete_session(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a session"""
        if session_id in self.sessions:
            del self.sessions[session_id]
            return True
        return False
    
    def cleanup_expired_sessions(self):
        """Remove expired sessions"""
        current_time = datetime.now()
        expired_sessions = []
        
        for session_id, session in self.sessions.items():
            if current_time - session.created_at > self.session_timeout:
                expired_sessions.append(session_id)
        
        for session_id in expired_sessions:
            self.delete_session(session_id)
    
    def get_gl_data(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:
        """Get GL DataFrame for a session"""
        session = self.get_session(session_id)
        return session.gl_data if session else None
    
    def get_ar_data(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:
        """Get AR DataFrame for a session"""
        session = self.get_session(session_id)
        return session.ar_data if session else None
    
    def set_gl_data(self, session_id: str, df: pd.DataFrame, filename: str) -> bool:
        """Set GL DataFrame for a session"""
        return self.update_session(session_id, gl_data=df, gl_filename=filename)
    
    def set_ar_data(self, session_id: str, df: pd.DataFrame, filename: str) -> bool:
        """Set AR DataFrame for a session"""
        return self.update_session(session_id, ar_data=df, ar_filename=filename)


# NOTE: Global session manager instance is created in shared_instances.py to avoid duplicates

Shared instances for the web application
"""

# Create a single shared session manager instance
session_manager = SessionManager()
